Implement the following plan:

# Fix: Google/Gemini via OpenRouter rejects tool schemas (GitHub #716)

## Context

When a user selects a Google/Gemini model through OpenRouter (e.g.
`google/gemma-4-31b-it:free`), the agent loop crashes with HTTP 400
`INVALID_ARGUMENT` because Google's API rejects our strict-mode-patched
tool schemas.

**Root cause**: `to_openai_tools()` unconditionally applies
`patch_schema_for_strict_mode()` + `strict: true`. The strict patch:

1. Forces ALL properties into the `required` array
2. Makes originally-optional properties nullable via `"type": ["boolean", "null"]`
3. Adds `additionalProperties: false` to every object

Google's native API (reached via OpenRouter) does not support array-form
types like `["boolean", "null"]`. It considers properties with those types
as "not defined", then rejects the `required` array for referencing them.
The cron tool schema triggers this because it has many optional boolean/enum
properties inside its nested `job` object.

**Why strict mode can be safely skipped for OpenRouter**: Strict mode is
an OpenAI-specific feature that prevents the model from hallucinating extra
JSON keys. OpenRouter proxies to many backends (Google, Anthropic, Meta,
etc.) and handles format translation itself. Passing `strict: true` through
OpenRouter to non-OpenAI backends causes validation errors without
providing any benefit — each upstream provider has its own schema
validation.

## Approach: parameterize strict mode in `to_openai_tools`

Add a `strict: bool` parameter to `to_openai_tools()`. When `false`, skip
`patch_schema_for_strict_mode()` and set `strict: false` in the tool
payload. Add a `needs_strict_tools()` method to `OpenAiProvider` that
returns `false` for OpenRouter (and Gemini direct, which uses the same
codepath and has the same limitation).

### Files to modify

1. **`crates/providers/src/openai_compat/mod.rs`**
   - `to_openai_tools(tools, strict: bool)` — skip `patch_schema_for_strict_mode` and set `strict` field accordingly
   - Update test calls at the bottom of the file

2. **`crates/providers/src/openai/provider/request.rs`**
   - Add `pub(super) fn needs_strict_tools(&self) -> bool` — returns `false` when `base_url` contains `"openrouter.ai"` or `provider_name` is `"gemini"`

3. **`crates/providers/src/openai/provider/completion.rs`** (line 228)
   - `to_openai_tools(tools)` → `to_openai_tools(tools, self.needs_strict_tools())`

4. **`crates/providers/src/openai/provider/streaming.rs`** (line 176)
   - Same change for `stream_with_tools_sse`

5. **`crates/providers/src/kimi_code.rs`** (lines 217, 312, 532)
   - Pass `true` — Kimi Code is its own provider and supports strict

6. **`crates/providers/src/github_copilot/mod.rs`** (lines 548, 891, 1225)
   - Pass `true` — GitHub Copilot targets OpenAI models that support strict

### New unit tests (in `crates/providers/src/openai_compat/mod.rs`)

7. **`to_openai_tools_strict_mode_applied_by_default`** — verifies that
   `to_openai_tools(tools, true)` still produces `strict: true`,
   `additionalProperties: false`, and all-properties-in-required. This is
   a regression guard for the OpenAI/Copilot/Kimi paths.

8. **`to_openai_tools_non_strict_skips_patching`** — verifies that
   `to_openai_tools(tools, false)` produces `strict: false`, preserves
   the original `required` array, and does NOT add `additionalProperties`
   or nullable types. Uses a schema with optional properties to prove they
   stay in their original form.

9. **`to_openai_tools_non_strict_complex_cron_like_schema`** — uses a
   schema modeled after the cron tool's `job` property (nested objects,
   union `type` arrays, optional booleans) and verifies it round-trips
   through `to_openai_tools(_, false)` without producing array-form types
   that Google rejects.

### New e2e tests (in `crates/providers/tests/openrouter_integration.rs`)

10. **`tool_call_google_model_non_streaming()`** — uses
    `google/gemma-4-31b-it:free` with a complex nested tool schema
    (mimicking the cron tool: nested objects, optional booleans, union
    types). Validates that the request doesn't 400.

11. **`tool_call_google_model_streaming()`** — same test, streaming path.

12. **Existing OpenRouter tests unchanged** — `tool_call_round_trip_*`
    and `multi_turn_tool_use` continue testing the `openai/gpt-4o-mini`
    model, confirming the standard OpenAI path still works.

## Verification

```bash
# Unit tests — strict/non-strict behavior
cargo test -p moltis-providers -- openai_compat

# Integration: OpenRouter + OpenAI model (existing, regression)
OPENROUTER_API_KEY=... cargo test --test openrouter_integration -- --ignored tool_call_round_trip

# Integration: OpenRouter + Google model (new, fix validation)
OPENROUTER_API_KEY=... cargo test --test openrouter_integration -- --ignored tool_call_google

# Full lint + test
just lint && just test
```

```
    23→    pub(super) fn stream_responses_sse(
    24→        &self,
    25→        messages: Vec<ChatMessage>,
    26→        tools: Vec<serde_json::Value>,
    27→    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Stream<Item = StreamEvent> + Send + '_>> {

    30→            let mut body = serde_json::json!({

    32→                "input": input,

    34→            });
    35→
    36→            if let Some(instructions) = instructions {
    37→                body["instructions"] = serde_json::Value::String(instructions);
    38→            }
    39→

    41→                body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_responses_api_tools(&tools));
    42→                body["tool_choice"] = serde_json::json!("auto");
    43→            }
    44→
    45→            self.apply_reasoning_effort_responses(&mut body);
    46→
    47→            debug!(
    48→                model = %self.model,
    49→                tools_count = tools.len(),
    50→                "openai stream_responses_sse request"
    51→            );
    52→            trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "openai responses stream request body");
    53→
    54→            let url = self.responses_sse_url();
    55→            let resp = match self
    56→                .client
    57→                .post(&url)
    58→                .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret()))
    59→                .header("content-type", "application/json")
    60→                .json(&body)
    61→                .send()
    62→                .await
    63→            {
    64→                Ok(r) => {
    65→                    if let Err(e) = r.error_for_status_ref() {
    66→                        let status = e.status().map(|s| s.as_u16()).unwrap_or(0);
    67→                        let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(r.headers());
    68→                        let body_text = r.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
    69→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(with_retry_after_marker(
    70→                            format!("HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
    71→                            retry_after_ms,
    72→                        ));
    73→                        return;
    74→                    }
    75→                    r
    76→                }
    77→                Err(e) => {
    78→                    yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
    79→                    return;
    80→                }
    81→            };
    82→
    83→            let mut byte_stream = resp.bytes_stream();
    84→            let mut buf = String::new();
    85→            let mut state = ResponsesStreamState::default();
    86→            let mut stream_done = false;
    87→
    88→            while let Some(chunk) = byte_stream.next().await {
    89→                let chunk = match chunk {
    90→                    Ok(c) => c,
    91→                    Err(e) => {
    92→                        yield StreamEvent::Error(e.to_string());
    93→                        return;
    94→                    }
    95→                };
    96→                buf.push_str(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&chunk));
    97→
    98→                while let Some(pos) = buf.find('\n') {
    99→                    let line = buf[..pos].trim().to_string();
   100→                    buf = buf[pos + 1..].to_string();
   101→
   102→                    if line.is_empty() {
   103→                        continue;
   104→                    }
   105→
   106→                    let Some(data) = line
   107→                        .strip_prefix("data: ")
   108→                        .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   109→                    else {
   110→                        // Handle bare event types (e.g. "event: response.completed")
   111→                        continue;
   112→                    };
   113→
   114→                    match process_responses_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   115→                        SseLineResult::Done => {
   116→                            stream_done = true;
   117→                            break;
   118→                        }
   119→                        SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   120→                            for event in events {
   121→                                yield event;
   122→                            }
   123→                        }
   124→                        SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   125→                    }
   126→                }
   127→                if stream_done {
   128→                    break;
   129→                }
   130→            }
   131→
   132→            // Process any residual buffered line on EOF.
   133→            if !stream_done {
   134→                let line = buf.trim().to_string();
   135→                if !line.is_empty()
   136→                    && let Some(data) = line
   137→                        .strip_prefix("data: ")
   138→                        .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   139→                {
   140→                    match process_responses_sse_line(data, &mut state) {
   141→                        SseLineResult::Done | SseLineResult::Skip => {}
   142→                        SseLineResult::Events(events) => {
   143→                            for event in events {
   144→                                yield event;
   145→                            }
   146→                        }
   147→                    }
   148→                }
   149→            }
   150→
   151→            // Finalize: emit pending ToolCallComplete events + Done with usage.
   152→            for event in finalize_responses_stream(&mut state) {
   153→                yield event;
   154→            }
   155→        })
   156→    }
```

```
     1→use secrecy::ExposeSecret;
     2→
     3→use tracing::{debug, trace, warn};
     4→
     5→use crate::{
     6→    http::{retry_after_ms_from_headers, with_retry_after_marker},
     7→    ollama::normalize_ollama_api_base_url,
     8→    openai_compat::{
     9→        parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload, parse_tool_calls,
    10→        split_responses_instructions_and_input, strip_think_tags, to_openai_tools,
    11→        to_responses_api_tools,
    12→    },
    13→    raw_model_id,
    14→};
    15→
    16→use moltis_agents::model::{ChatMessage, CompletionResponse, Usage};
    17→
    18→use super::OpenAiProvider;
    19→
    20→fn is_chat_endpoint_unsupported_model_error(body_text: &str) -> bool {
    21→    let lower = body_text.to_ascii_lowercase();
    22→    lower.contains("not a chat model")
    23→        || lower.contains("does not support chat")
    24→        || lower.contains("only supported in v1/responses")
    25→        || lower.contains("not supported in the v1/chat/completions endpoint")
    26→        || lower.contains("input content or output modality contain audio")
    27→        || lower.contains("requires audio")
    28→}
    29→
    30→fn should_warn_on_api_error(status: reqwest::StatusCode, body_text: &str) -> bool {
    31→    if is_chat_endpoint_unsupported_model_error(body_text) {
    32→        return false;
    33→    }
    34→    !matches!(status.as_u16(), 404)
    35→}
    36→
    37→impl OpenAiProvider {
    38→    fn apply_probe_output_cap_chat(&self, body: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    39→        let raw = raw_model_id(&self.model).to_ascii_lowercase();
    40→        let capability = raw.rsplit('/').next().unwrap_or(raw.as_str());
    41→        let uses_max_completion_tokens = capability.starts_with("gpt-5")
    42→            || capability.starts_with("o1")
    43→            || capability.starts_with("o3")
    44→            || capability.starts_with("o4");
    45→        if uses_max_completion_tokens {
    46→            // GPT-5 and reasoning models need a higher minimum output cap.
    47→            // Values below ~10 can trigger 400 errors on some models.
    48→            body["max_completion_tokens"] = serde_json::json!(16);
    49→        } else {
    50→            body["max_tokens"] = serde_json::json!(1);
    51→        }
    52→    }
    53→
    54→    pub(super) async fn probe_chat_completions(&self) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    55→        let messages = vec![ChatMessage::user("ping")];
    56→        let mut openai_messages = self.serialize_messages_for_request(&messages);
    57→        self.apply_openrouter_cache_control(&mut openai_messages);
    58→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
    59→            "model": self.model,
    60→            "messages": openai_messages,
    61→        });
    62→        self.apply_system_prompt_rewrite(&mut body);
    63→        // Probes only answer "can this model respond at all?".
    64→        // Keep them cheap instead of mirroring full reasoning budgets.
    65→        self.apply_probe_output_cap_chat(&mut body);
    66→
    67→        debug!(model = %self.model, "openai probe request");
    68→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "openai probe request body");
    69→
    70→        let http_resp = self
    71→            .client
    72→            .post(format!("{}/chat/completions", self.base_url))
    73→            .header(
    74→                "Authorization",
    75→                format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret()),
    76→            )
    77→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
    78→            .json(&body)
    79→            .send()
    80→            .await?;
    81→
    82→        let status = http_resp.status();
    83→        if !status.is_success() {
    84→            let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
    85→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
    86→            if should_warn_on_api_error(status, &body_text) {
    87→                warn!(
    88→                    status = %status,
    89→                    model = %self.model,
    90→                    provider = %self.provider_name,
    91→                    body = %body_text,
    92→                    "openai probe API error"
    93→                );
    94→            } else {
    95→                debug!(
    96→                    status = %status,
    97→                    model = %self.model,
    98→                    provider = %self.provider_name,
    99→                    "openai probe model unsupported for chat/completions endpoint"
   100→                );
   101→            }
   102→            // Ollama's OpenAI-compat layer returns 404 for models that
   103→            // exist but aren't wired to /v1/chat/completions.  Fall back
   104→            // to the native `/api/show` endpoint before giving up.
   105→            if status == reqwest::StatusCode::NOT_FOUND
   106→                && self.provider_name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("ollama")
   107→            {
   108→                return self.probe_ollama_native().await;
   109→            }
   110→
   111→            anyhow::bail!(
   112→                "{}",
   113→                with_retry_after_marker(
   114→                    format!("OpenAI API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   115→                    retry_after_ms,
   116→                )
   117→            );
   118→        }
   119→
   120→        Ok(())
   121→    }
   122→
   123→    /// Fallback probe for Ollama: POST `/api/show` with the model name.
   124→    ///
   125→    /// This confirms the model is installed and Ollama is reachable even when
   126→    /// the OpenAI-compat `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint returns 404.
   127→    async fn probe_ollama_native(&self) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   128→        let api_base = normalize_ollama_api_base_url(&self.base_url);
   129→        let url = format!("{}/api/show", api_base.trim_end_matches('/'));
   130→
   131→        debug!(model = %self.model, url = %url, "ollama native probe via /api/show");
   132→
   133→        let mut req = self
   134→            .client
   135→            .post(&url)
   136→            .json(&serde_json::json!({ "name": self.model }));
   137→        let key = self.api_key.expose_secret();
   138→        if !key.is_empty() {
   139→            req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {key}"));
   140→        }
   141→        let resp = req.send().await?;
   142→
   143→        if resp.status().is_success() {
   144→            return Ok(());
   145→        }
   146→
   147→        let status = resp.status();
   148→        let body_text = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   149→        anyhow::bail!(
   150→            "Model '{}' not found. Make sure it is installed (ollama pull {}) \
   151→             and try again. (Ollama /api/show returned HTTP {}: {})",
   152→            self.model,
   153→            self.model,
   154→            status,
   155→            body_text,
   156→        )
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    pub(super) async fn probe_responses(&self) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
   160→        let messages = vec![ChatMessage::user("ping")];
   161→        let (instructions, input) = split_responses_instructions_and_input(messages);
   162→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   163→            "model": self.model,
   164→            "input": input,
   165→            "max_output_tokens": 1,
   166→        });
   167→
   168→        if let Some(instructions) = instructions {
   169→            body["instructions"] = serde_json::Value::String(instructions);
   170→        }
   171→
   172→        self.apply_reasoning_effort_responses(&mut body);
   173→
   174→        debug!(model = %self.model, "openai responses probe request");
   175→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "openai responses probe request body");
   176→
   177→        let url = self.responses_sse_url();
   178→        let http_resp = self
   179→            .client
   180→            .post(&url)
   181→            .header(
   182→                "Authorization",
   183→                format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret()),
   184→            )
   185→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   186→            .json(&body)
   187→            .send()
   188→            .await?;
   189→
   190→        let status = http_resp.status();
   191→        if !status.is_success() {
   192→            let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   193→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   194→            warn!(
   195→                status = %status,
   196→                model = %self.model,
   197→                provider = %self.provider_name,
   198→                body = %body_text,
   199→                "openai responses probe API error"
   200→            );
   201→            anyhow::bail!(
   202→                "{}",
   203→                with_retry_after_marker(
   204→                    format!("OpenAI API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   205→                    retry_after_ms,
   206→                )
   207→            );
   208→        }
   209→
   210→        Ok(())
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    /// Non-streaming completion using the Chat Completions API.
   214→    pub(super) async fn complete_chat(
   215→        &self,
   216→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   217→        tools: &[serde_json::Value],
   218→    ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   219→        let mut openai_messages = self.serialize_messages_for_request(messages);
   220→        self.apply_openrouter_cache_control(&mut openai_messages);
   221→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   222→            "model": self.model,
   223→            "messages": openai_messages,
   224→        });
   225→        self.apply_system_prompt_rewrite(&mut body);
   226→
   227→        if !tools.is_empty() {
   228→            body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_openai_tools(tools));
   229→        }
   230→
   231→        self.apply_reasoning_effort_chat(&mut body);
   232→
   233→        debug!(
   234→            model = %self.model,
   235→            messages_count = messages.len(),
   236→            tools_count = tools.len(),
   237→            reasoning_effort = ?self.reasoning_effort,
   238→            "openai complete request"
   239→        );
   240→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "openai request body");
   241→
   242→        let http_resp = self
   243→            .client
   244→            .post(format!("{}/chat/completions", self.base_url))
   245→            .header(
   246→                "Authorization",
   247→                format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret()),
   248→            )
   249→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   250→            .json(&body)
   251→            .send()
   252→            .await?;
   253→
   254→        let status = http_resp.status();
   255→        if !status.is_success() {
   256→            let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   257→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   258→            if should_warn_on_api_error(status, &body_text) {
   259→                warn!(
   260→                    status = %status,
   261→                    model = %self.model,
   262→                    provider = %self.provider_name,
   263→                    body = %body_text,
   264→                    "openai API error"
   265→                );
   266→            } else {
   267→                debug!(
   268→                    status = %status,
   269→                    model = %self.model,
   270→                    provider = %self.provider_name,
   271→                    "openai model unsupported for chat/completions endpoint"
   272→                );
   273→            }
   274→            anyhow::bail!(
   275→                "{}",
   276→                with_retry_after_marker(
   277→                    format!("OpenAI API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   278→                    retry_after_ms,
   279→                )
   280→            );
   281→        }
   282→
   283→        let resp = http_resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await?;
   284→        trace!(response = %resp, "openai raw response");
   285→
   286→        let message = &resp["choices"][0]["message"];
   287→
   288→        let text = message["content"].as_str().and_then(|s| {
   289→            let (visible, _thinking) = strip_think_tags(s);
   290→            if visible.is_empty() {
   291→                None
   292→            } else {
   293→                Some(visible)
   294→            }
   295→        });
   296→        let tool_calls = parse_tool_calls(message);
   297→
   298→        let usage = parse_openai_compat_usage_from_payload(&resp).unwrap_or_default();
   299→
   300→        Ok(CompletionResponse {
   301→            text,
   302→            tool_calls,
   303→            usage,
   304→        })
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    /// Non-streaming completion using the Responses API.
   308→    ///
   309→    /// Sends `stream: true` and collects events into a single response, since
   310→    /// many Responses API endpoints only support streaming.
   311→    pub(super) async fn complete_responses(
   312→        &self,
   313→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   314→        tools: &[serde_json::Value],
   315→    ) -> anyhow::Result<CompletionResponse> {
   316→        let (instructions, input) = split_responses_instructions_and_input(messages.to_vec());
   317→        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
   318→            "model": self.model,
   319→            "input": input,
   320→            "stream": true,
   321→        });
   322→        if let Some(instructions) = instructions {
   323→            body["instructions"] = serde_json::Value::String(instructions);
   324→        }
   325→        if !tools.is_empty() {
   326→            body["tools"] = serde_json::Value::Array(to_responses_api_tools(tools));
   327→            body["tool_choice"] = serde_json::json!("auto");
   328→        }
   329→
   330→        debug!(
   331→            model = %self.model,
   332→            messages_count = messages.len(),
   333→            tools_count = tools.len(),
   334→            "openai complete_responses request"
   335→        );
   336→        trace!(body = %serde_json::to_string(&body).unwrap_or_default(), "openai responses request body");
   337→
   338→        let url = self.responses_sse_url();
   339→        let http_resp = self
   340→            .client
   341→            .post(&url)
   342→            .header(
   343→                "Authorization",
   344→                format!("Bearer {}", self.api_key.expose_secret()),
   345→            )
   346→            .header("content-type", "application/json")
   347→            .json(&body)
   348→            .send()
   349→            .await?;
   350→
   351→        let status = http_resp.status();
   352→        if !status.is_success() {
   353→            let retry_after_ms = retry_after_ms_from_headers(http_resp.headers());
   354→            let body_text = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   355→            anyhow::bail!(
   356→                "{}",
   357→                with_retry_after_marker(
   358→                    format!("Responses API error HTTP {status}: {body_text}"),
   359→                    retry_after_ms,
   360→                )
   361→            );
   362→        }
   363→
   364→        // Collect SSE events into text + tool calls.
   365→        let mut text_buf = String::new();
   366→        let mut fn_call_ids: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   367→        let mut fn_call_names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   368→        let mut fn_call_args: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   369→        let mut input_tokens: u32 = 0;
   370→        let mut output_tokens: u32 = 0;
   371→        let mut cache_read_tokens: u32 = 0;
   372→        let cache_write_tokens: u32 = 0;
   373→
   374→        let full_body = http_resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   375→        for line in full_body.lines() {
   376→            let line = line.trim();
   377→            if line.is_empty() {
   378→                continue;
   379→            }
   380→            let Some(data) = line
   381→                .strip_prefix("data: ")
   382→                .or_else(|| line.strip_prefix("data:"))
   383→            else {
   384→                continue;
   385→            };
   386→            if data == "[DONE]" {
   387→                break;
   388→            }
   389→
   390→            let Ok(evt) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(data) else {
   391→                continue;
   392→            };
   393→
   394→            match evt["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("") {
   395→                "response.output_text.delta" => {
   396→                    if let Some(delta) = evt["delta"].as_str() {
   397→                        text_buf.push_str(delta);
   398→                    }
   399→                },
   400→                "response.output_item.added" => {
   401→                    if evt["item"]["type"].as_str() == Some("function_call") {
   402→                        fn_call_ids.push(evt["item"]["call_id"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string());
   403→                        fn_call_names.push(evt["item"]["name"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string());
   404→                        fn_call_args.push(String::new());
   405→                    }
   406→                },
   407→                "response.function_call_arguments.delta" => {
   408→                    if let Some(delta) = evt["delta"].as_str()
   409→                        && let Some(last) = fn_call_args.last_mut()
   410→                    {
   411→                        last.push_str(delta);
   412→                    }
   413→                },
   414→                "response.completed" => {
   415→                    if let Some(u) = evt["response"]["usage"].as_object() {
   416→                        input_tokens =
   417→                            u.get("input_tokens").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()).unwrap_or(0) as u32;
   418→                        output_tokens =
   419→                            u.get("output_tokens").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()).unwrap_or(0) as u32;
   420→                        cache_read_tokens = u
   421→                            .get("input_tokens_details")
   422→                            .and_then(|d| d.get("cached_tokens"))
   423→                            .and_then(|v| v.as_u64())
   424→                            .unwrap_or(0) as u32;
   425→                    }
   426→                },
   427→                "error" | "response.failed" => {
   428→                    let msg = evt["error"]["message"]
   429→                        .as_str()
   430→                        .or_else(|| evt["response"]["error"]["message"].as_str())
   431→                        .or_else(|| evt["message"].as_str())
   432→                        .unwrap_or("unknown error");
   433→                    anyhow::bail!("Responses API error: {msg}");
   434→                },
   435→                _ => {},
   436→            }
   437→        }
   438→
   439→        let text = if text_buf.is_empty() {
   440→            None
   441→        } else {
   442→            Some(text_buf)
   443→        };
   444→
   445→        let tool_calls: Vec<moltis_agents::model::ToolCall> = fn_call_ids
   446→            .into_iter()
   447→            .zip(fn_call_names)
   448→            .zip(fn_call_args)
   449→            .filter_map(|((id, name), args)| {
   450→                let arguments: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&args)
   451→                    .unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Object(Default::default()));
   452→                if name.is_empty() {
   453→                    return None;
   454→                }
   455→                Some(moltis_agents::model::ToolCall {
   456→                    id,
   457→                    name,
   458→                    arguments,
   459→                })
   460→            })
   461→            .collect();
   462→
   463→        Ok(CompletionResponse {
   464→            text,
   465→            tool_calls,
   466→            usage: Usage {
   467→                input_tokens,
   468→                output_tokens,
   469→                cache_read_tokens,
   470→                cache_write_tokens,
   471→            },
   472→        })
   473→    }
   474→}
   475→
```

```
     1→use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
     2→
     3→use tracing::warn;
     4→
     5→use moltis_agents::model::ChatMessage;
     6→
     7→use super::OpenAiProvider;
     8→
     9→impl OpenAiProvider {
    10→    /// Returns `true` when this provider targets an Anthropic model via
    11→    /// OpenRouter, which supports prompt caching when `cache_control`
    12→    /// breakpoints are present in the message payload.
    13→    fn is_openrouter_anthropic(&self) -> bool {
    14→        self.base_url.contains("openrouter.ai") && self.model.starts_with("anthropic/")
    15→    }
    16→
    17→    /// For OpenRouter Anthropic models, inject `cache_control` breakpoints
    18→    /// on the system message and the last user message to enable prompt
    19→    /// caching passthrough to Anthropic.
    20→    pub(super) fn apply_openrouter_cache_control(&self, messages: &mut [serde_json::Value]) {
    21→        if !self.is_openrouter_anthropic()
    22→            || matches!(self.cache_retention, moltis_config::CacheRetention::None)
    23→        {
    24→            return;
    25→        }
    26→
    27→        let cache_control = serde_json::json!({ "type": "ephemeral" });
    28→
    29→        // Add cache_control to the system message content.
    30→        for msg in messages.iter_mut() {
    31→            if msg.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) != Some("system") {
    32→                continue;
    33→            }
    34→            match msg.get_mut("content") {
    35→                Some(content) if content.is_string() => {
    36→                    let text = content.as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
    37→                    msg["content"] = serde_json::json!([{
    38→                        "type": "text",
    39→                        "text": text,
    40→                        "cache_control": cache_control
    41→                    }]);
    42→                },
    43→                Some(content) if content.is_array() => {
    44→                    if let Some(last) = content.as_array_mut().and_then(|a| a.last_mut()) {
    45→                        last["cache_control"] = cache_control.clone();
    46→                    }
    47→                },
    48→                _ => {},
    49→            }
    50→            break;
    51→        }
    52→
    53→        // Add cache_control to the last user message.
    54→        if let Some(last_user) = messages
    55→            .iter_mut()
    56→            .rev()
    57→            .find(|m| m.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("user"))
    58→        {
    59→            match last_user.get_mut("content") {
    60→                Some(content) if content.is_string() => {
    61→                    let text = content.as_str().unwrap_or_default().to_string();
    62→                    last_user["content"] = serde_json::json!([{
    63→                        "type": "text",
    64→                        "text": text,
    65→                        "cache_control": cache_control
    66→                    }]);
    67→                },
    68→                Some(content) if content.is_array() => {
    69→                    if let Some(last) = content.as_array_mut().and_then(|a| a.last_mut()) {
    70→                        last["cache_control"] = cache_control;
    71→                    }
    72→                },
    73→                _ => {},
    74→            }
    75→        }
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    fn requires_reasoning_content_on_tool_messages(&self) -> bool {
    79→        self.provider_name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("moonshot")
    80→            || self.base_url.contains("moonshot.ai")
    81→            || self.base_url.contains("moonshot.cn")
    82→            || self.model.starts_with("kimi-")
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    /// Some providers (e.g. MiniMax) reject `role: "system"` in the messages
    86→    /// array. System content must be extracted and prepended to the first user
    87→    /// message instead (MiniMax silently ignores a top-level `"system"` field).
    88→    fn rejects_system_role(&self) -> bool {
    89→        self.model.starts_with("MiniMax-")
    90→            || self.provider_name.eq_ignore_ascii_case("minimax")
    91→            || self.base_url.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("minimax")
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    /// For providers that reject `role: "system"` in the messages array,
    95→    /// extract all system messages from `body["messages"]`, join their
    96→    /// content, and prepend it to the first user message.
    97→    ///
    98→    /// MiniMax's `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint returns error 2013 for
    99→    /// `role: "system"` entries and silently ignores a top-level `"system"`
   100→    /// field. The only reliable way to deliver the system prompt is to
   101→    /// inline it into the first user message.
   102→    ///
   103→    /// Must be called on the request body **after** it is fully assembled.
   104→    pub(super) fn apply_system_prompt_rewrite(&self, body: &mut serde_json::Value) {
   105→        if !self.rejects_system_role() {
   106→            return;
   107→        }
   108→        let Some(messages) = body
   109→            .get_mut("messages")
   110→            .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array_mut)
   111→        else {
   112→            return;
   113→        };
   114→        let mut system_parts = Vec::new();
   115→        messages.retain(|msg| {
   116→            if msg.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("system") {
   117→                if let Some(content) = msg.get("content").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   118→                    && !content.is_empty()
   119→                {
   120→                    system_parts.push(content.to_string());
   121→                } else if msg.get("content").is_some() {
   122→                    warn!("MiniMax system message has non-string content; it will be dropped");
   123→                }
   124→                return false;
   125→            }
   126→            true
   127→        });
   128→        if system_parts.is_empty() {
   129→            return;
   130→        }
   131→        let system_text = system_parts.join("\n\n");
   132→
   133→        // Find the first user message and prepend system content to it.
   134→        let system_block =
   135→            format!("[System Instructions]\n{system_text}\n[End System Instructions]\n\n");
   136→        if let Some(first_user) = messages
   137→            .iter_mut()
   138→            .find(|m| m.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("user"))
   139→        {
   140→            match first_user.get("content").cloned() {
   141→                Some(serde_json::Value::String(s)) => {
   142→                    first_user["content"] = serde_json::Value::String(format!("{system_block}{s}"));
   143→                },
   144→                Some(serde_json::Value::Array(mut arr)) => {
   145→                    // Multimodal content (text + images): prepend as a text block.
   146→                    arr.insert(
   147→                        0,
   148→                        serde_json::json!({ "type": "text", "text": system_block }),
   149→                    );
   150→                    first_user["content"] = serde_json::Value::Array(arr);
   151→                },
   152→                _ => {
   153→                    first_user["content"] = serde_json::Value::String(system_block);
   154→                },
   155→            }
   156→        } else {
   157→            // No user message yet (e.g. probe); insert a synthetic user message.
   158→            messages.insert(
   159→                0,
   160→                serde_json::json!({
   161→                    "role": "user",
   162→                    "content": format!("[System Instructions]\n{system_text}\n[End System Instructions]")
   163→                }),
   164→            );
   165→        }
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    pub(super) fn serialize_messages_for_request(
   169→        &self,
   170→        messages: &[ChatMessage],
   171→    ) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   172→        let needs_reasoning_content = self.requires_reasoning_content_on_tool_messages();
   173→        let mut remapped_tool_call_ids = HashMap::new();
   174→        let mut used_tool_call_ids = HashSet::new();
   175→        let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(messages.len());
   176→
   177→        for message in messages {
   178→            let mut value = message.to_openai_value();
   179→
   180→            if let Some(tool_calls) = value
   181→                .get_mut("tool_calls")
   182→                .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array_mut)
   183→            {
   184→                for tool_call in tool_calls {
   185→                    let Some(tool_call_id) =
   186→                        tool_call.get("id").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   187→                    else {
   188→                        continue;
   189→                    };
   190→                    let mapped_id = assign_openai_tool_call_id(
   191→                        tool_call_id,
   192→                        &mut remapped_tool_call_ids,
   193→                        &mut used_tool_call_ids,
   194→                    );
   195→                    tool_call["id"] = serde_json::Value::String(mapped_id);
   196→                }
   197→            } else if value.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("tool")
   198→                && let Some(tool_call_id) = value
   199→                    .get("tool_call_id")
   200→                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   201→            {
   202→                let mapped_id = remapped_tool_call_ids
   203→                    .get(tool_call_id)
   204→                    .cloned()
   205→                    .unwrap_or_else(|| {
   206→                        assign_openai_tool_call_id(
   207→                            tool_call_id,
   208→                            &mut remapped_tool_call_ids,
   209→                            &mut used_tool_call_ids,
   210→                        )
   211→                    });
   212→                value["tool_call_id"] = serde_json::Value::String(mapped_id);
   213→            }
   214→
   215→            if needs_reasoning_content {
   216→                let is_assistant =
   217→                    value.get("role").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str) == Some("assistant");
   218→                let has_tool_calls = value
   219→                    .get("tool_calls")
   220→                    .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   221→                    .is_some_and(|calls| !calls.is_empty());
   222→
   223→                if is_assistant && has_tool_calls {
   224→                    let reasoning_content = value
   225→                        .get("content")
   226→                        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)
   227→                        .unwrap_or("")
   228→                        .to_string();
   229→
   230→                    if value.get("content").is_none() {
   231→                        value["content"] = serde_json::Value::String(String::new());
   232→                    }
   233→
   234→                    if value.get("reasoning_content").is_none() {
   235→                        value["reasoning_content"] = serde_json::Value::String(reasoning_content);
   236→                    }
   237→                }
   238→            }
   239→
   240→            out.push(value);
   241→        }
   242→
   243→        out
   244→    }
   245→}
   246→
   247→const OPENAI_MAX_TOOL_CALL_ID_LEN: usize = 40;
   248→
   249→fn short_stable_hash(value: &str) -> String {
   250→    use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
   251→
   252→    let mut hasher = std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher::new();
   253→    value.hash(&mut hasher);
   254→    format!("{:016x}", hasher.finish())
   255→}
   256→
   257→fn base_openai_tool_call_id(raw: &str) -> String {
   258→    let mut cleaned: String = raw
   259→        .chars()
   260→        .map(|ch| {
   261→            if ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(ch, '_' | '-') {
   262→                ch
   263→            } else {
   264→                '_'
   265→            }
   266→        })
   267→        .collect();
   268→
   269→    if cleaned.is_empty() {
   270→        cleaned = "call".to_string();
   271→    }
   272→
   273→    if cleaned.len() <= OPENAI_MAX_TOOL_CALL_ID_LEN {
   274→        return cleaned;
   275→    }
   276→
   277→    let hash = short_stable_hash(raw);
   278→    let keep = OPENAI_MAX_TOOL_CALL_ID_LEN.saturating_sub(hash.len() + 1);
   279→    cleaned.truncate(keep);
   280→    if cleaned.is_empty() {
   281→        return format!("call-{hash}");
   282→    }
   283→    format!("{cleaned}-{hash}")
   284→}
   285→
   286→fn disambiguate_tool_call_id(base: &str, nonce: usize) -> String {
   287→    let suffix = format!("-{nonce}");
   288→    let keep = OPENAI_MAX_TOOL_CALL_ID_LEN.saturating_sub(suffix.len());
   289→
   290→    let mut value = base.to_string();
   291→    if value.len() > keep {
   292→        value.truncate(keep);
   293→    }
   294→    if value.is_empty() {
   295→        value = "call".to_string();
   296→        if value.len() > keep {
   297→            value.truncate(keep);
   298→        }
   299→    }
   300→    format!("{value}{suffix}")
   301→}
   302→
   303→fn assign_openai_tool_call_id(
   304→    raw: &str,
   305→    remapped_tool_call_ids: &mut HashMap<String, String>,
   306→    used_tool_call_ids: &mut HashSet<String>,
   307→) -> String {
   308→    if let Some(existing) = remapped_tool_call_ids.get(raw) {
   309→        return existing.clone();
   310→    }
   311→
   312→    let base = base_openai_tool_call_id(raw);
   313→    let mut candidate = base.clone();
   314→    let mut nonce = 1usize;
   315→    while used_tool_call_ids.contains(&candidate) {
   316→        candidate = disambiguate_tool_call_id(&base, nonce);
   317→        nonce = nonce.saturating_add(1);
   318→    }
   319→
   320→    used_tool_call_ids.insert(candidate.clone());
   321→    remapped_tool_call_ids.insert(raw.to_string(), candidate.clone());
   322→    candidate
   323→}
   324→
```

    69→fn make_nullable(schema: &mut serde_json::Value) {

    90→        } else if let Some(arr) = ty.as_array() {
    91→            let mut new_arr = arr.clone();
    92→            new_arr.push(serde_json::json!("null"));
    93→            obj.insert("type".to_string(), serde_json::Value::Array(new_arr));
    94→        }
    95→        return;
    96→    }
    97→
    98→    // Case 2: uses anyOf/oneOf — append a null variant.

   111→
   112→/// Recursively patch schema for OpenAI strict mode compliance.
   113→///
   114→/// OpenAI's strict mode requires:
   115→/// 1. `additionalProperties: false` on every object in the schema tree
   116→/// 2. All properties must be listed in the `required` array
   117→///
   118→/// Properties not in the original `required` array are made nullable so the
   119→/// model can send `null` for unused optional fields instead of fabricating
   120→/// placeholder values.
   121→///
   122→/// This function recursively patches nested objects in `properties`, array
   123→/// `items`, `anyOf`/`oneOf`/`allOf` variants, etc.
   124→pub fn patch_schema_for_strict_mode(schema: &mut serde_json::Value) {
   125→    let Some(obj) = schema.as_object_mut() else {
   126→        return;
   127→    };
   128→
   129→    // Collect originally-optional property names so we can make them nullable
   130→    // AFTER recursion (otherwise changing "type":"object" to ["object","null"]
   131→    // would prevent the recursive pass from recognising nested objects).
   132→    let mut optional_props: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   133→
   134→    // If this is an object type, apply strict mode requirements
   135→    if obj.get("type").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) == Some("object") {
   136→        // Add additionalProperties: false
   137→        obj.insert("additionalProperties".to_string(), serde_json::json!(false));
   138→
   139→        // Ensure all properties are in required array.
   140→        if let Some(props) = obj.get("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object()).cloned() {
   141→            let originally_required: HashSet<String> = obj
   142→                .get("required")
   143→                .and_then(|r| r.as_array())
   144→                .map(|arr| {
   145→                    arr.iter()
   146→                        .filter_map(|v| v.as_str().map(String::from))
   147→                        .collect()
   148→                })
   149→                .unwrap_or_default();
   150→
   151→            let all_prop_names: Vec<serde_json::Value> =
   152→                props.keys().map(|k| serde_json::json!(k)).collect();
   153→            obj.insert("required".to_string(), serde_json::json!(all_prop_names));
   154→
   155→            // Remember which properties need to be made nullable (deferred).
   156→            for key in props.keys() {
   157→                if !originally_required.contains(key) {
   158→                    optional_props.push(key.clone());
   159→                }
   160→            }
   161→        } else {
   162→            // Object without properties - add empty properties and required
   163→            obj.insert("properties".to_string(), serde_json::json!({}));
   164→            obj.insert("required".to_string(), serde_json::json!([]));
   165→        }
   166→    }
   167→
   168→    // Recurse into properties
   169→    if let Some(props) = obj.get_mut("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object_mut()) {
   170→        for (_, prop_schema) in props.iter_mut() {
   171→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(prop_schema);
   172→        }
   173→    }
   174→
   175→    // Recurse into array items
   176→    if let Some(items) = obj.get_mut("items") {
   177→        patch_schema_for_strict_mode(items);
   178→    }
   179→
   180→    // Recurse into anyOf/oneOf/allOf
   181→    for key in ["anyOf", "oneOf", "allOf"] {
   182→        if let Some(variants) = obj.get_mut(key).and_then(|v| v.as_array_mut()) {
   183→            for variant in variants {
   184→                patch_schema_for_strict_mode(variant);
   185→            }
   186→        }
   187→    }
   188→
   189→    // Recurse into additionalProperties if it's a schema (not just true/false)
   190→    if let Some(additional) = obj.get_mut("additionalProperties")
   191→        && additional.is_object()
   192→    {
   193→        patch_schema_for_strict_mode(additional);
   194→    }
   195→
   196→    // Now that recursion is done, make originally-optional properties nullable
   197→    // so the model can send `null` instead of fabricating placeholder values
   198→    // (e.g. empty strings) that downstream MCP servers reject.
   199→    if !optional_props.is_empty()
   200→        && let Some(props) = obj.get_mut("properties").and_then(|p| p.as_object_mut())
   201→    {
   202→        for key in &optional_props {
   203→            if let Some(prop_schema) = props.get_mut(key) {
   204→                make_nullable(prop_schema);
   205→            }
   206→        }
   207→    }
   208→}
   209→
   210→/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Chat Completions function-calling format.
   211→///
   212→/// Uses the nested `function` object format required by Chat Completions API:
   213→/// ```json
   214→/// { "type": "function", "function": { "name": "...", ... } }
   215→/// ```
   216→///
   217→/// Adds `strict: true` and patches schemas for strict mode compliance:
   218→/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas
   219→/// - All properties included in `required` array
   220→///
   221→/// This is required by some APIs (Claude via Copilot) to ensure the model
   222→/// provides all required fields.
   223→///
   224→/// See: <https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling>
   225→pub fn to_openai_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   226→    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools
   227→        .iter()
   228→        .filter_map(|t| {
   229→            // Clone parameters and patch for strict mode
   230→            let mut params = t["parameters"].clone();
   231→            sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat(&mut params);
   232→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);
   233→
   234→            let name = t["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   235→            let description = t["description"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   236→
   237→            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present
   238→            let tool = ChatCompletionsTool {
   239→                tool_type: "function",
   240→                function: ChatCompletionsFunction {
   241→                    name: name.clone(),
   242→                    description,
   243→                    parameters: params,
   244→                    strict: true,
   245→                },
   246→            };
   247→
   248→            trace!(tool_name = %name, "converted tool to Chat Completions format");
   249→
   250→            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API
   251→            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()
   252→        })
   253→        .collect();
   254→
   255→    trace!(tools_count = result.len(), "to_openai_tools complete");
   256→    result
   257→}
   258→
   259→/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Responses API function-calling format.
   260→///
   261→/// Uses the flat format required by the Responses API where `name` is at top level:
   262→/// ```json
   263→/// { "type": "function", "name": "...", "parameters": {...}, "strict": true }
   264→/// ```
   265→///
   266→/// This is the format used by OpenAI Codex and the Responses API.
   267→///
   268→/// Patches schemas for strict mode compliance:
   269→/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas
   270→/// - All properties included in `required` array
   271→///
   272→/// See: <https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/ai-foundry/openai/how-to/responses>
   273→pub fn to_responses_api_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   274→    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools
   275→        .iter()
   276→        .filter_map(|t| {
   277→            // Clone parameters and patch for strict mode
   278→            let mut params = t["parameters"].clone();
   279→            sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat(&mut params);
   280→            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);
   281→
   282→            let name = t["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   283→            let description = t["description"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
   284→
   285→            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present
   286→            let tool = ResponsesApiTool {
   287→                tool_type: "function",
   288→                name: name.clone(),
   289→                description,
   290→                parameters: params,
   291→                strict: true,
   292→            };
   293→
   294→            trace!(tool_name = %name, "converted tool to Responses API format");
   295→
   296→            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API
   297→            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()
   298→        })
   299→        .collect();

```
    18→    model: String,
    19→    base_url: String,
    20→    provider_name: String,
    21→    client: &'static reqwest::Client,
    22→    stream_transport: ProviderStreamTransport,
    23→    wire_api: WireApi,
    24→    metadata_cache: tokio::sync::OnceCell<ModelMetadata>,
    25→    tool_mode_override: Option<moltis_config::ToolMode>,
    26→    /// Optional reasoning effort level for o-series models.
    27→    reasoning_effort: Option<moltis_agents::model::ReasoningEffort>,
    28→    /// Prompt cache retention policy (used for OpenRouter Anthropic passthrough).
    29→    cache_retention: moltis_config::CacheRetention,
    30→}

    32→const OPENAI_MODELS_ENDPOINT_PATH: &str = "/models";
    33→
    34→#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
    35→struct ModelCatalogEntry {
    36→    id: &'static str,
    37→    display_name: &'static str,
    38→}
    39→
    40→impl ModelCatalogEntry {
    41→    const fn new(id: &'static str, display_name: &'static str) -> Self {
    42→        Self { id, display_name }

    44→}
    45→
    46→const DEFAULT_OPENAI_MODELS: &[ModelCatalogEntry] = &[
    47→    ModelCatalogEntry::new("gpt-5.2", "GPT-5.2"),
    48→    ModelCatalogEntry::new("gpt-5.2-chat-latest", "GPT-5.2 Chat Latest"),
    49→    ModelCatalogEntry::new("gpt-5-mini", "GPT-5 Mini"),
    50→];
    51→
    52→#[must_use]
    53→pub fn default_model_catalog() -> Vec<DiscoveredModel> {
    54→    DEFAULT_OPENAI_MODELS
    55→        .iter()
    56→        .map(|entry| {
    57→            DiscoveredModel::new(entry.id, entry.display_name)
    58→                .with_recommended(is_recommended_openai_model(entry.id))
    59→        })
    60→        .collect()
```

```
   300→
   301→    trace!(
   302→        tools_count = result.len(),
   303→        "to_responses_api_tools complete"

   305→    result
   306→}
   307→
   308→/// Convert typed chat messages to Responses API input items.
   309→///
   310→/// Responses API accepts a heterogeneous input array (messages, tool calls, and
   311→/// tool outputs). This keeps one canonical conversion for providers that use
   312→/// Responses transport (SSE or WebSocket).
   313→#[must_use]
   314→pub fn to_responses_input(messages: &[ChatMessage]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
   315→    messages
   316→        .iter()
   317→        .flat_map(|msg| match msg {
   318→            ChatMessage::System { .. } => {
   319→                // System messages are extracted into `instructions`.
   320→                vec![]
   321→            },
   322→            ChatMessage::User { content } => {
   323→                let content_blocks = match content {
   324→                    UserContent::Text(t) => {
   325→                        vec![serde_json::json!({"type": "input_text", "text": t})]
   326→                    },
   327→                    UserContent::Multimodal(parts) => parts
   328→                        .iter()
   329→                        .map(|p| match p {
   330→                            moltis_agents::model::ContentPart::Text(t) => {
   331→                                serde_json::json!({"type": "input_text", "text": t})
   332→                            },
   333→                            moltis_agents::model::ContentPart::Image { media_type, data } => {
   334→                                let data_uri = format!("data:{media_type};base64,{data}");
   335→                                serde_json::json!({
   336→                                    "type": "input_image",
   337→                                    "image_url": data_uri,
   338→                                })
   339→                            },
   340→                        })
   341→                        .collect(),
   342→                };
   343→                vec![serde_json::json!({
   344→                    "role": "user",
   345→                    "content": content_blocks,
   346→                })]
   347→            },
   348→            ChatMessage::Assistant {
   349→                content,
   350→                tool_calls,
   351→            } => {
   352→                if !tool_calls.is_empty() {
   353→                    let mut items: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tool_calls
   354→                        .iter()
   355→                        .map(|tc| {
   356→                            serde_json::json!({
   357→                                "type": "function_call",
   358→                                "call_id": tc.id,
   359→                                "name": tc.name,
   360→                                "arguments": tc.arguments.to_string(),
   361→                            })
   362→                        })
   363→                        .collect();
   364→                    if let Some(text) = content
   365→                        && !text.is_empty()
   366→                    {
   367→                        items.insert(
   368→                            0,
   369→                            serde_json::json!({
   370→                                "type": "message",
   371→                                "role": "assistant",
   372→                                "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": text}]
   373→                            }),
   374→                        );
   375→                    }
   376→                    items
   377→                } else {
   378→                    let text = content.as_deref().unwrap_or("");
   379→                    vec![serde_json::json!({
   380→                        "type": "message",
   381→                        "role": "assistant",
   382→                        "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": text}]
   383→                    })]
   384→                }
   385→            },
   386→            ChatMessage::Tool {
   387→                tool_call_id,
   388→                content,
   389→            } => {
   390→                vec![serde_json::json!({
   391→                    "type": "function_call_output",
   392→                    "call_id": tool_call_id,
   393→                    "output": content,
   394→                })]
   395→            },
   396→        })
   397→        .collect()
   398→}
   399→
   400→/// Parse tool_calls from an OpenAI response message (non-streaming).
   401→pub fn parse_tool_calls(message: &serde_json::Value) -> Vec<ToolCall> {
   402→    message["tool_calls"]
   403→        .as_array()
   404→        .map(|tcs| {
   405→            tcs.iter()
   406→                .filter_map(|tc| {
   407→                    let id = tc["id"].as_str()?.to_string();
   408→                    let name = tc["function"]["name"].as_str()?.to_string();
   409→                    let arguments = decode_tool_call_arguments(tc["function"].get("arguments"));
   410→                    Some(ToolCall {
   411→                        id,
   412→                        name,
   413→                        arguments,
   414→                    })
   415→                })
   416→                .collect()
   417→        })
   418→        .unwrap_or_default()
   419→}
   420→
   421→fn usage_value_at_path(usage: &serde_json::Value, path: &[&str]) -> Option<u64> {
   422→    let mut cursor = usage;
   423→    for key in path {
   424→        cursor = cursor.get(*key)?;
   425→    }
   426→    cursor
   427→        .as_u64()
   428→        .or_else(|| cursor.as_str().and_then(|raw| raw.parse::<u64>().ok()))
   429→}
   430→
   431→fn usage_field_u32(usage: &serde_json::Value, paths: &[&[&str]]) -> u32 {
   432→    paths
   433→        .iter()
   434→        .find_map(|path| usage_value_at_path(usage, path))
   435→        .unwrap_or(0) as u32
   436→}
   437→
   438→fn usage_object_from_payload(payload: &serde_json::Value) -> Option<&serde_json::Value> {
   439→    if let Some(usage) = payload.get("usage").filter(|usage| usage.is_object()) {
   440→        return Some(usage);
   441→    }
   442→
   443→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   444→        .get("choices")
   445→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   446→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   447→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("usage"))
   448→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   449→    {
   450→        return Some(usage);
   451→    }
   452→
   453→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   454→        .get("choices")
   455→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   456→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   457→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("delta"))
   458→        .and_then(|delta| delta.get("usage"))
   459→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   460→    {
   461→        return Some(usage);
   462→    }
   463→
   464→    if let Some(usage) = payload
   465→        .get("choices")
   466→        .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_array)
   467→        .and_then(|choices| choices.first())
   468→        .and_then(|choice| choice.get("message"))
   469→        .and_then(|message| message.get("usage"))
   470→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   471→    {
   472→        return Some(usage);
   473→    }
   474→
   475→    payload
   476→        .get("x_groq")
   477→        .and_then(|x_groq| x_groq.get("usage"))
   478→        .filter(|usage| usage.is_object())
   479→}
   480→
   481→/// Parse usage payloads from OpenAI-compatible backends.
   482→///
   483→/// Different providers use different field names:
   484→/// - OpenAI-style: `prompt_tokens`, `completion_tokens`
   485→/// - Anthropic/MiniMax-style: `input_tokens`, `output_tokens`
   486→/// - Cache fields may be top-level or nested in `*_tokens_details`.
   487→#[must_use]
   488→pub fn parse_openai_compat_usage(usage: &serde_json::Value) -> Usage {
   489→    Usage {
   490→        input_tokens: usage_field_u32(usage, &[
   491→            &["prompt_tokens"],
   492→            &["promptTokens"],
   493→            &["input_tokens"],
   494→            &["inputTokens"],
   495→        ]),
   496→        output_tokens: usage_field_u32(usage, &[
   497→            &["completion_tokens"],
   498→            &["completionTokens"],
   499→            &["output_tokens"],
```

```
     1→//! Live integration tests for the OpenRouter provider.
     2→//!
     3→//! Requires `OPENROUTER_API_KEY`. Run with:
     4→//!   cargo test --test openrouter_integration -- --ignored
     5→//!
     6→//! OpenRouter is a model router — it proxies requests to upstream providers.
     7→//! We test with a cheap, reliable model to validate the integration path.
     8→
     9→#![allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]
    10→
    11→use {
    12→    futures::StreamExt,
    13→    moltis_agents::model::{ChatMessage, LlmProvider, StreamEvent, ToolCall},
    14→    moltis_providers::openai::OpenAiProvider,
    15→    secrecy::{ExposeSecret, Secret},
    16→};
    17→
    18→const BASE_URL: &str = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1";
    19→/// Use a cheap model for testing. OpenRouter has no static catalog — models
    20→/// are discovered via API.
    21→const TEST_MODEL: &str = "openai/gpt-4o-mini";
    22→
    23→fn api_key() -> Secret<String> {
    24→    Secret::new(
    25→        std::env::var("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
    26→            .expect("OPENROUTER_API_KEY must be set for integration tests"),
    27→    )
    28→}
    29→
    30→fn make_provider(model: &str) -> OpenAiProvider {
    31→    OpenAiProvider::new_with_name(
    32→        api_key(),
    33→        model.to_string(),
    34→        BASE_URL.to_string(),
    35→        "openrouter".to_string(),
    36→    )
    37→}
    38→
    39→fn weather_tool() -> serde_json::Value {
    40→    serde_json::json!({
    41→        "name": "get_weather",
    42→        "description": "Get current weather for a location. You MUST call this tool when asked about weather.",
    43→        "parameters": {
    44→            "type": "object",
    45→            "properties": {
    46→                "location": { "type": "string", "description": "City name" }
    47→            },
    48→            "required": ["location"]
    49→        }
    50→    })
    51→}
    52→
    53→// ── System prompt ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    54→
    55→#[tokio::test]
    56→#[ignore]
    57→async fn system_prompt_is_received_non_streaming() {
    58→    let p = make_provider(TEST_MODEL);
    59→    let keyword = "PERSIMMON";
    60→    let messages = vec![
    61→        ChatMessage::system(format!(
    62→            "You MUST include the exact word \"{keyword}\" in every response, no matter what."
    63→        )),
    64→        ChatMessage::user("What is 2+2?"),
    65→    ];
    66→    let response = p.complete(&messages, &[]).await.expect("should succeed");
    67→    let text = response.text.expect("must have text");
    68→    assert!(
    69→        text.to_lowercase().contains(&keyword.to_lowercase()),
    70→        "system prompt not received: {text:?}"
    71→    );
    72→}
    73→
    74→#[tokio::test]
    75→#[ignore]
    76→async fn system_prompt_is_received_streaming() {
    77→    let p = make_provider(TEST_MODEL);
    78→    let keyword = "GUAVA";
    79→    let messages = vec![
    80→        ChatMessage::system(format!(
    81→            "You MUST include the exact word \"{keyword}\" in every response, no matter what."
    82→        )),
    83→        ChatMessage::user("What is 3+3?"),
    84→    ];
    85→    let mut stream = p.stream_with_tools(messages, vec![]);
    86→    let mut full_text = String::new();
    87→    let mut saw_done = false;
    88→    while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
    89→        match event {
    90→            StreamEvent::Delta(chunk) => full_text.push_str(&chunk),
    91→            StreamEvent::Done(_) => {
    92→                saw_done = true;
    93→                break;
    94→            },
    95→            StreamEvent::Error(err) => panic!("stream error: {err}"),
    96→            _ => {},
    97→        }
    98→    }
    99→    assert!(saw_done, "stream must emit Done");
   100→    assert!(
   101→        full_text.to_lowercase().contains(&keyword.to_lowercase()),
   102→        "system prompt not received: {full_text:?}"
   103→    );
   104→}
   105→
   106→// ── Tool calling ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   107→
   108→#[tokio::test]
   109→#[ignore]
   110→async fn tool_call_round_trip_non_streaming() {
   111→    let p = make_provider(TEST_MODEL);
   112→    let response = p
   113→        .complete(
   114→            &[ChatMessage::user(
   115→                "What's the weather in Tokyo? Use the get_weather tool.",

   128→
   129→#[tokio::test]
   130→#[ignore]
   131→async fn tool_call_round_trip_streaming() {
   132→    let p = make_provider(TEST_MODEL);
   133→    let mut stream = p.stream_with_tools(
   134→        vec![ChatMessage::user(
   135→            "What's the weather in Paris? Use the get_weather tool.",
   136→        )],
   137→        vec![weather_tool()],
   138→    );
   139→    let mut saw_tool = false;
   140→    let mut saw_done = false;
   141→    let mut tool_name = String::new();
   142→    while let Some(event) = stream.next().await {
   143→        match event {
   144→            StreamEvent::ToolCallStart { name, .. } => {
   145→                saw_tool = true;

   151→            },
   152→            StreamEvent::Error(err) => panic!("stream error: {err}"),

   154→        }
   155→    }
   156→    assert!(saw_done, "must emit Done");
   157→    assert!(saw_tool, "should include tool call");
   158→    assert_eq!(tool_name, "get_weather");

   160→
   161→#[tokio::test]
   162→#[ignore]
   163→async fn multi_turn_tool_use() {

   165→    let tools = vec![weather_tool()];

   167→        .complete(

   182→                    arguments: tc.arguments.clone(),
   183→                }]),

   185→            ],
   186→            &tools,
   187→        )
   188→        .await
   189→        .expect("second turn");
   190→    assert!(r2.text.is_some(), "should have text after tool result");
   191→}
   192→
   193→// ── Probe & streaming ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

   203→

   206→async fn stream_emits_delta_and_done() {

   208→    let mut stream = p.stream(vec![ChatMessage::user("Say hello in one word.")]);
   209→    let mut saw_delta = false;
```

```
  1261→    fn sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat_strips_recursive_unsupported_keywords() {
  1262→        let mut schema = serde_json::json!({
  1263→            "type": "object",
  1264→            "properties": {
  1265→                "config": {
  1266→                    "type": "object",
  1267→                    "properties": {
  1268→                        "mode": { "type": "string" }
  1269→                    },
  1270→                    "if": {

  1272→                    },

  1275→                            "enabled": { "type": "boolean" }
  1276→                        }
  1277→                    },
  1278→                    "else": {
  1279→                        "properties": {
  1280→                            "enabled": { "type": "boolean" }
  1281→                        }
  1282→                    },
  1283→                    "dependentSchemas": {
  1284→                        "mode": {
  1285→                            "properties": {
  1286→                                "extra": { "type": "string" }
  1287→                            }
  1288→                        }
  1289→                    },
  1290→                    "patternProperties": {
  1291→                        "^x-": { "type": "string" }
  1292→                    },
  1293→                    "dependentRequired": {
  1294→                        "mode": ["enabled"]
  1295→                    },
  1296→                    "unevaluatedProperties": false,
  1297→                    "unevaluatedItems": false,
  1298→                    "propertyNames": {
  1299→                        "minLength": 1
  1300→                    },
  1301→                    "contains": {
  1302→                        "type": "string"
  1303→                    },
  1304→                    "minContains": 1,
  1305→                    "maxContains": 2,
  1306→                    "minProperties": 1,
  1307→                    "maxProperties": 4,
  1308→                    "const": "active",
  1309→                    "x-custom": "remove-me",
  1310→                    "items": {

  1312→                            "type": "integer"
  1313→                        }
  1314→                    }
  1315→                }
  1316→            }
  1317→        });

  1319→        sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat(&mut schema);
  1320→        let encoded = schema.to_string();

  1324→            "\"then\"",

  1341→        }
```

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/.superset/worktrees/moltis/glowing-snowplow/crates/providers/src/openai_compat/mod.rs",
  "old_string": "/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Chat Completions function-calling format.\n///\n/// Uses the nested `function` object format required by Chat Completions API:\n/// ```json\n/// { \"type\": \"function\", \"function\": { \"name\": \"...\", ... } }\n/// ```\n///\n/// Adds `strict: true` and patches schemas for strict mode compliance:\n/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas\n/// - All properties included in `required` array\n///\n/// This is required by some APIs (Claude via Copilot) to ensure the model\n/// provides all required fields.\n///\n/// See: <https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling>\npub fn to_openai_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value]) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {\n    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools\n        .iter()\n        .filter_map(|t| {\n            // Clone parameters and patch for strict mode\n            let mut params = t[\"parameters\"].clone();\n            sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat(&mut params);\n            patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);\n\n            let name = t[\"name\"].as_str()?.to_string();\n            let description = t[\"description\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string();\n\n            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present\n            let tool = ChatCompletionsTool {\n                tool_type: \"function\",\n                function: ChatCompletionsFunction {\n                    name: name.clone(),\n                    description,\n                    parameters: params,\n                    strict: true,\n                },\n            };\n\n            trace!(tool_name = %name, \"converted tool to Chat Completions format\");\n\n            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API\n            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()\n        })\n        .collect();\n\n    trace!(tools_count = result.len(), \"to_openai_tools complete\");\n    result\n}",
  "new_string": "/// Convert tool schemas to OpenAI Chat Completions function-calling format.\n///\n/// Uses the nested `function` object format required by Chat Completions API:\n/// ```json\n/// { \"type\": \"function\", \"function\": { \"name\": \"...\", ... } }\n/// ```\n///\n/// When `strict` is `true`, patches schemas for strict mode compliance:\n/// - `additionalProperties: false` on all object schemas\n/// - All properties included in `required` array\n/// - Originally-optional properties made nullable via array-form types\n///\n/// When `strict` is `false`, schemas are sanitized but not patched for strict\n/// mode. This avoids array-form types like `[\"boolean\", \"null\"]` that\n/// non-OpenAI backends (e.g. Google via OpenRouter) reject.\n///\n/// See: <https://platform.openai.com/docs/guides/function-calling>\npub fn to_openai_tools(tools: &[serde_json::Value], strict: bool) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {\n    let result: Vec<serde_json::Value> = tools\n        .iter()\n        .filter_map(|t| {\n            let mut params = t[\"parameters\"].clone();\n            sanitize_schema_for_openai_compat(&mut params);\n            if strict {\n                patch_schema_for_strict_mode(&mut params);\n            }\n\n            let name = t[\"name\"].as_str()?.to_string();\n            let description = t[\"description\"].as_str().unwrap_or(\"\").to_string();\n\n            // Use typed struct to ensure all required fields are present\n            let tool = ChatCompletionsTool {\n                tool_type: \"function\",\n                function: ChatCompletionsFunction {\n                    name: name.clone(),\n                    description,\n                    parameters: params,\n                    strict,\n                },\n            };\n\n            trace!(tool_name = %name, strict, \"converted tool to Chat Completions format\");\n\n            // Serialize to Value for compatibility with existing API\n            serde_json::to_value(tool).ok()\n        })\n        .collect();\n\n    trace!(tools_count = result.len(), \"to_openai_tools complete\");\n    result\n}"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "    /// Returns `true` when tool schemas should use OpenAI strict mode.\n    ///\n    /// Strict mode is an OpenAI-specific feature that adds `additionalProperties:\n    /// false` and forces all properties into the `required` array (making\n    /// originally-optional ones nullable via array-form types like\n    /// `[\"boolean\", \"null\"]`).\n    ///\n    /// Non-OpenAI backends reached through OpenRouter (Google, Anthropic, Meta,\n    /// etc.) and the Gemini direct provider reject these array-form types.\n    /// OpenRouter handles format translation itself, so strict mode provides no\n    /// benefit there for non-OpenAI upstream models.\n    pub(super) fn needs_strict_tools(&self) -> bool {\n        if self.base_url.contains(\"openrouter.ai\") {\n            return false;\n        }\n        if self.provider_name.eq_ignore_ascii_case(\"gemini\") {\n            return false;\n        }\n        true\n    }\n\n    fn requires_reasoning_content_on_tool_messages(&self) -> bool {"
}
```
